Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the mol_bio space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 143 and the convergence rate is O(576/n^1).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on mol_bio structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 143. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 576. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the mol_bio object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 437. The moduli space has dimension at most 787 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 437 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the mol_bio structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 787. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
